def _longwave_primary(sh,vs,vb,sun,shade,solid,sine,cosine,directions,gate,solar_gate,sky_down,sky_side,surface_sun,surface_sh,lup,reflection_factor):

    # --- LINE 94 --- 
    #   $6load_attr.1 = getattr(value=sh, attr=shape)  :: UniTuple(int64 x 2)
    #   $26unpack_sequence.4 = exhaust_iter(value=$6load_attr.1, count=2)  :: UniTuple(int64 x 2)
    #   del $6load_attr.1
    #   $pixels.82 = static_getitem(value=$26unpack_sequence.4, index=0, index_var=None, fn=<built-in function getitem>)  :: int64
    #   $patches.81 = static_getitem(value=$26unpack_sequence.4, index=1, index_var=None, fn=<built-in function getitem>)  :: int64
    #   del $26unpack_sequence.4

    pixels,patches=sh.shape

    # --- LINE 95 --- 
    #   $const66.9.1 = const(int, 7)  :: Literal[int](7)
    #   id=1[LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.60, range = (0, sh_size0.1, 1)), LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.61, range = (0, $const66.9.1, 1))]{2791: <ir.Block at /Users/alansynn/Workspace/solweig-light/src/solweig_light/radiation/cylinder_longwave.py (95)>}Var($parfor_index_tuple_var.67, cylinder_longwave.py:95)

    output=np.zeros((pixels,7),dtype=np.float32)

    # --- LINE 96 --- 
    #   id=3[LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.73, range = (0, $pixels.82, 1))]{288: <ir.Block at /Users/alansynn/Workspace/solweig-light/src/solweig_light/radiation/cylinder_longwave.py (99)>,     #   del vs
    #   del vb
    #   del surface_sun
    #   del surface_sh
    #   del sun
    #   del solid
    #   del solar_gate
    #   del sky_side
    #   del sky_down
    #   del sine
    #   del shade
    #   del sh
    #   del reflection_factor
    #   del parfor_index.68
    #   del parfor_index.58
    #   del lup
    #   del cosine
    #   del $patches.81

    for pixel in prange(pixels):

        # --- LINE 97 --- 

        accum=np.zeros(10,dtype=np.float32)

        # --- LINE 98 --- 

        for patch in range(patches):

            # --- LINE 99 --- 

            sky=sh[pixel,patch]==1 and vs[pixel,patch]==1

            # --- LINE 100 --- 

            veg=vs[pixel,patch]==0 or vb[pixel,patch]==0

            # --- LINE 101 --- 

            building=np.float32(np.float32(1)-sh[pixel,patch])*vb[pixel,patch]==1

            # --- LINE 102 --- 

            accum[0]=np.float32(accum[0]+np.float32(sky*sky_down[patch]))

            # --- LINE 103 --- 

            accum[5]=np.float32(accum[5]+np.float32(sky*sky_side[patch]))

            # --- LINE 104 --- 

            vegetation_side=((surface_sh*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*veg

            # --- LINE 105 --- 

            vegetation_down=((surface_sh*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*veg

            # --- LINE 106 --- 

            accum[6]=np.float32(accum[6]+vegetation_side)

            # --- LINE 107 --- 

            accum[1]=np.float32(accum[1]+vegetation_down)

            # --- LINE 108 --- 

            if solar_gate[patch]:

                # --- LINE 109 --- 

                sun_side=((((surface_sun*sun[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 110 --- 

                shade_side=((((surface_sh*shade[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 111 --- 

                sun_down=((((surface_sun*sun[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 112 --- 

                shade_down=((((surface_sh*shade[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 113 --- 

                accum[8]=np.float32(accum[8]+sun_side)

                # --- LINE 114 --- 

                accum[7]=np.float32(accum[7]+shade_side)

                # --- LINE 115 --- 

                accum[3]=np.float32(accum[3]+sun_down)

                # --- LINE 116 --- 

                accum[2]=np.float32(accum[2]+shade_down)

            # --- LINE 117 --- 

            else:

                # --- LINE 118 --- 

                shade_side=(((surface_sh*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 119 --- 

                shade_down=(((surface_sh*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 120 --- 

                accum[7]=np.float32(accum[7]+shade_side)

                # --- LINE 121 --- 

                accum[2]=np.float32(accum[2]+shade_down)

        # --- LINE 122 --- 

        # The reflection field depends on the completed ordered sky sweep.

        # --- LINE 123 --- 

        reflected=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[0]+lup[pixel])*reflection_factor)*np.float32(.5))/np.float32(np.pi))

        # --- LINE 124 --- 

        for patch in range(patches):

            # --- LINE 125 --- 

            mask=sh[pixel,patch]==0 or vs[pixel,patch]==0 or vb[pixel,patch]==0

            # --- LINE 126 --- 

            side=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(reflected*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*mask)

            # --- LINE 127 --- 

            down=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(reflected*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*mask)

            # --- LINE 128 --- 

            accum[9]=np.float32(accum[9]+side)

            # --- LINE 129 --- 

            accum[4]=np.float32(accum[4]+down)

        # --- LINE 130 --- 

        output[pixel,0]=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[0]+accum[1])+accum[2])+accum[3])+accum[4])

        # --- LINE 131 --- 

        output[pixel,1]=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[5]+accum[6])+accum[7])+accum[8])+accum[9])

        # --- LINE 132 --- 

        output[pixel,2:7]=accum[5:10]

    # --- LINE 133 --- 
    #   $2792return_value.3 = cast(value=output)  :: array(float32, 2d, C)
    #   del output
    #   return $2792return_value.3

    return output

    #   $6load_attr.1 = getattr(value=sh, attr=shape)  :: UniTuple(int64 x 2)
    #   $26unpack_sequence.4 = exhaust_iter(value=$6load_attr.1, count=2)  :: UniTuple(int64 x 2)
    #   del $6load_attr.1
    #   $pixels.380 = static_getitem(value=$26unpack_sequence.4, index=0, index_var=None, fn=<built-in function getitem>)  :: int64
    #   $patches.381 = static_getitem(value=$26unpack_sequence.4, index=1, index_var=None, fn=<built-in function getitem>)  :: int64
    #   del $26unpack_sequence.4

    pixels,patches=sh.shape

    # --- LINE 95 --- 
    #   $const66.9.1 = const(int, 7)  :: Literal[int](7)
    #   id=5[LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.359, range = (0, sh_size0.300, 1)), LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.360, range = (0, $const66.9.1, 1))]{2989: <ir.Block at /Users/alansynn/Workspace/solweig-light/src/solweig_light/radiation/cylinder_longwave.py (95)>}Var($parfor_index_tuple_var.366, cylinder_longwave.py:95)

    output=np.zeros((pixels,7),dtype=np.float32)

    # --- LINE 96 --- 
    #   id=7[LoopNest(index_variable = parfor_index.372, range = (0, $pixels.380, 1))]{288: <ir.Block at /Users/alansynn/Workspace/solweig-light/src/solweig_light/radiation/cylinder_longwave.py (99)>,     #   del vs
    #   del vb
    #   del surface_sun
    #   del surface_sh
    #   del sun
    #   del solid
    #   del solar_gate
    #   del sky_side
    #   del sky_down
    #   del sine
    #   del shade
    #   del sh
    #   del reflection_factor
    #   del parfor_index.367
    #   del parfor_index.357
    #   del lup
    #   del cosine
    #   del $patches.381

    for pixel in prange(pixels):

        # --- LINE 97 --- 

        accum=np.zeros(10,dtype=np.float32)

        # --- LINE 98 --- 

        for patch in range(patches):

            # --- LINE 99 --- 

            sky=sh[pixel,patch]==1 and vs[pixel,patch]==1

            # --- LINE 100 --- 

            veg=vs[pixel,patch]==0 or vb[pixel,patch]==0

            # --- LINE 101 --- 

            building=np.float32(np.float32(1)-sh[pixel,patch])*vb[pixel,patch]==1

            # --- LINE 102 --- 

            accum[0]=np.float32(accum[0]+np.float32(sky*sky_down[patch]))

            # --- LINE 103 --- 

            accum[5]=np.float32(accum[5]+np.float32(sky*sky_side[patch]))

            # --- LINE 104 --- 

            vegetation_side=((surface_sh*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*veg

            # --- LINE 105 --- 

            vegetation_down=((surface_sh*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*veg

            # --- LINE 106 --- 

            accum[6]=np.float32(accum[6]+vegetation_side)

            # --- LINE 107 --- 

            accum[1]=np.float32(accum[1]+vegetation_down)

            # --- LINE 108 --- 

            if solar_gate[patch]:

                # --- LINE 109 --- 

                sun_side=((((surface_sun*sun[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 110 --- 

                shade_side=((((surface_sh*shade[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 111 --- 

                sun_down=((((surface_sun*sun[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 112 --- 

                shade_down=((((surface_sh*shade[pixel,patch])*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 113 --- 

                accum[8]=np.float32(accum[8]+sun_side)

                # --- LINE 114 --- 

                accum[7]=np.float32(accum[7]+shade_side)

                # --- LINE 115 --- 

                accum[3]=np.float32(accum[3]+sun_down)

                # --- LINE 116 --- 

                accum[2]=np.float32(accum[2]+shade_down)

            # --- LINE 117 --- 

            else:

                # --- LINE 118 --- 

                shade_side=(((surface_sh*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 119 --- 

                shade_down=(((surface_sh*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*building)

                # --- LINE 120 --- 

                accum[7]=np.float32(accum[7]+shade_side)

                # --- LINE 121 --- 

                accum[2]=np.float32(accum[2]+shade_down)

        # --- LINE 122 --- 

        # The reflection field depends on the completed ordered sky sweep.

        # --- LINE 123 --- 

        reflected=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[0]+lup[pixel])*reflection_factor)*np.float32(.5))/np.float32(np.pi))

        # --- LINE 124 --- 

        for patch in range(patches):

            # --- LINE 125 --- 

            mask=sh[pixel,patch]==0 or vs[pixel,patch]==0 or vb[pixel,patch]==0

            # --- LINE 126 --- 

            side=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(reflected*solid[patch])*cosine[patch])*mask)

            # --- LINE 127 --- 

            down=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(reflected*solid[patch])*sine[patch])*mask)

            # --- LINE 128 --- 

            accum[9]=np.float32(accum[9]+side)

            # --- LINE 129 --- 

            accum[4]=np.float32(accum[4]+down)

        # --- LINE 130 --- 

        output[pixel,0]=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[0]+accum[1])+accum[2])+accum[3])+accum[4])

        # --- LINE 131 --- 

        output[pixel,1]=np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(np.float32(accum[5]+accum[6])+accum[7])+accum[8])+accum[9])

        # --- LINE 132 --- 

        output[pixel,2:7]=accum[5:10]

    # --- LINE 133 --- 
    #   $2792return_value.3 = cast(value=output)  :: array(float32, 2d, C)
    #   del output
    #   return $2792return_value.3

    return output
